- **worktree-list**: List all active feature worktrees with their status. Shows branch name, commits ahead of main, and whether there are uncommitted changes. Use to get an overview when working with multiple parallel features. ([corbat-tech/coco](https://github.com/corbat-tech/coco/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/worktree-list) · ★1)
- **worktree-list**: List all git worktrees for this repository ([AndreMashukov/StudyForge](https://github.com/AndreMashukov/StudyForge/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/worktree-list) · 3 copies)
- **worktree-make-dev**: Set up a dev environment in an existing worktree (make dev + copy secrets) ([dlt-hub/dlt](https://github.com/dlt-hub/dlt/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/worktree-make-dev) · ★5.9k)
